│ [subtaskplanner] Plan to produce a concise end-to-end validation and results │
│ report for correctness and performance                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ Goal                                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • Produce a reproducible, end-to-end results report confirming correctness  │
│    and, where possible, performance targets for the C extension path across  │
│    sizes n = 1000, 5000, 8000.                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ What I will deliver                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • A one-page reproducibility report (Markdown) including:                   │
│     • Data seed and shapes                                                   │
│     • Per-size results for Python baseline and C extension (if available)    │
│     • Absolute diffs and tolerance check (target: <= 1e-10 when C path is    │
│       available)                                                             │
│     • Speedup summary for 5000+ assets (target: >= 1.2x)                     │
│     • Clear interpretation and next steps                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ Subtask plan (execution-ready)                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Subtask A: Run correctness validation                                     │
│     • Build the extension if possible (python3 setup.py build_ext --inplace) │
│     • Run the test_validation.py harness (or the equivalent in your repo)    │
│     • Collect per-size results:                                              │
│        • risk_py, ret_py (Python baseline)                                   │
│        • risk_c, ret_c (C path, if build succeeds)                           │
│        • diffs: |risk_c - risk_py|, |ret_c - ret_py|                         │
│     • Confirm: diffs <= 1e-10 when C path is available                       │
│  • Subtask B: Run performance validation (if build is available)             │
│     • Run benchmark.py to obtain timings for both paths                      │
│     • Compute speedups per size (C time / Python baseline time)              │
│     • Confirm: speedup >= 1.2x for n >= 5000                                 │
│     • If speedup is below target, note the delta and potential bottlenecks   │
│  • Subtask C: Produce the reproducibility report                             │
│     • Create a concise table with columns:                                   │
│        • Size (n)                                                            │
│        • risk_py                                                             │
│        • risk_c                                                              │
│        • diff_risk                                                           │
│        • ret_py                                                              │
│        • ret_c                                                               │
│        • diff_ret                                                            │
│        • c_available (yes/no)                                                │
│        • pass (yes/no for tolerance)                                         │
│        • If available, speedup (for 5000+ only)                              │
│     • Add a short interpretation section:                                    │
│        • Whether correctness passes                                          │
│        • Whether performance target is met                                   │
│        • Any caveats (e.g., environment constraints)                         │
│     • Include seed and data shapes as metadata                               │
│  • Subtask D: If targets aren’t met                                          │
│     • Propose and outline concrete next steps:                               │
│        • One-pass optimization refinements (already implemented; outline     │
│          additional micro-optimizations if needed)                           │
│        • Additional validation with a slightly altered data distribution to  │
│          stress numerics                                                     │
│        • Any minimal patch candidates and how to test them                   │
│  • Subtask E: Deliverables                                                   │
│     • The final reproducibility report (Markdown)                            │
│     • A short runbook you can reuse for future iterations                    │
│     • If helpful, a small CSV-friendly version of the results for easy       │
│       import into dashboards                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ Assumptions and caveats                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  • The C path availability depends on successful build tooling; if           │
│    unavailable, the report should clearly mark C path as unavailable and     │
│    rely on Python baseline results.                                          │
│  • Tolerance is strict (1e-10) for correctness when C path is present.       │
│  • Timing measurements can be noisy; include notes on system load and        │
│    run-to-run variability if you record multiple runs.                       │
│                                                                              │
│ What I need to proceed (optional)                                            │
│                                                                              │
│  • If you want, I can provide a ready-to-fill Markdown template for the      │
│    report, plus a minimal CSV/TSV appendix for easy integration with your    │
│    docs or dashboards.                                                       │
│  • If you’re ready to run now, I can tailor the exact report layout to your  │
│    preferred format (Markdown, reStructuredText, or CSV) and include         │
│    placeholder fields you can fill after running the tests.                  │
│                                                                              │
│ Status                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • Ready to execute subtask A–D as soon as you confirm you have or will      │
│    enable a build-enabled environment. If you prefer, I can also generate a  │
│    Git patch-style diff package with the required patches and a compact      │
│    runbook for your CI.                                                      │
